#7425 1 2006-03-14T02:37:05Z Unreality
Anyone playing N with a slow computer and feeling frustrated?

I play N with a slow computer.... N lags seriously especially on tense moments, maybe there are too many objects to test-hit. Skipping a few frames per few seconds happened a lot to me.

With a slow computer, I can hardly do any precise moves. Fortunately in most levels, there are always a safe (but slow) route for you to go. So my approach to N, is to find the route that minimize the skill required

With this in mind I'm able to pass most of the episode.. and I have around 20 episodes left to go.

Highscores? Never think about it. But finally one day when I reached 97-1, I found that it is one of the level that requires extremely few amounts of precise direction changing.... (on a slow computer you aren't likely to able to change your direction at the exact frame you want), so I go for it and luckily enough to get a 9th highscore.

And then I found that most of the scores in 9th column are "under-scored". Look at 97-3, many of the 97-3 highscores haven't cleared all gold...
(maybe this is due to some of the N players quit N before reaching the bottom half of the 9th column)

I love most part of N, but I have something to whine about.
1. I would like to have an option to switch RagDoll off..... the ragdoll made a lag computer more laggy, the ragdoll seems to consume your computer resources every times you die, and when you have been killed more, your computer became more laggy, and more lag leads to more dead.

2. Anyone find the bouncing box annoying?

Firstly, to jump at the desired height, you need to jump at the correct frame after landing on the bouncing box, which is very hard to do on a frame-skipping computer.

Secondly, the bouncing box is quite "inPhysics. Say, to suit the law of physics, "when the bouncing box is moving upward, the Ninja standing on it should always jump higher". But the result is "no, not always, sometimes you will jump much much lower when the bouncing box is bouncing you upwards", and I call this "inPhysics".
Another example, when you land on the bouncing box, then at the moment before it bounces back upward (i.e. the moment which the bouncing box is stationary), the ninja should able to jump at a normal height. But again the result is "no".... and I think that this is "inPhysics" too!

Although this is just a game, realistic is not required, and no one ever officially stated that the bouncing box suits the law of Physics, but I still feel quite annoying when the bouncing box contradict to my basic instinct.

#194624 310 2006-06-26T03:33:53Z Unreality
I'm a flash programmer and I think it is possible to detect the changes in flash speed by illegal application

try this
http://ssf.unrealize.com/ssf/help/smooth.swf
then switch on cheat engine or whatever else that changes the speed of flash.

When the speed of flash has changed illegally, the green bar inside the smooth.swf will increase the length and turn red in color.

it's possible to validate the highscore submission too, by getting a key from the server before a level start, get another key during the middle of the play, and then submitting the score & the speed of flash with keys encrypted. The server also needed to check the time of giving out the keys & the time of receiving the highscore.

#10470 1 2006-08-05T16:03:29Z Unreality
Okay here's a new N version with speedhack detection.

The URL of the file has been removed as we already got raigan's attention.

When this N program runs with a slower speed, it will automatically stop the game.

It has extremely low tolerance. If N runs with 90% slower speed, the program can detect it around 10 seconds. If N runs with 50% speed, the program can almost detect it instantly.

On the other hand, users with slow computer may find that any CPU lag will mislead the program thinks that you are using speedhack

The zip file contains two files,
- n_v14_shielded.exe
- speedhack_shield.dll

firstly, don't worry, the dll won't register itself into your computer, and the .exe is just a Flash-Windows-Projector.

secondly, you can't play the game if speedhack_shield.dll is not in the same directory with n_v14_shielded.exe



If you would like to give a help on the speedhack detection for N, please download my zip file and give feedback to me.........

Don't spread the url to other people tho... I want only serious players to test it... I don't want the public download the N game from my server......

and... if I have done anything violated the license.. please kindly tell me

=================================================

#213136 42 2006-08-08T10:44:39Z Unreality
I can't wait for those new levels, tho I haven't finished the original game yet

By the way how many of you are using flash player 8 to play N?

If you play N with flash player 8, here is a N mod for you: http://www.matrixoft.net/agents/calvin/n/n...2_drone_v01.swf
You can put this mod on the same folder of your 'flash player 8 N', and open this mod with flash player. Well this may be the first N mod ....

This N mod has the following features:
1. Thwump speed increased 50%
2. All Drones speed increased 100%
3. Laser Drone Pre-Fire-Delay decreased down to 15 frame
4. Laser Drone Post-Fire-Delay decreased down to 0 frame
5. Chaingun Drone Fire much more rapidly

While some original levels may not be play-able in this mode, but some other levels are 10 times more enjoyable now

Try level 01-0 , level 2-3, and then level 34-3 too, I'm sure you will like the fierce-ness of the drones

About this mod:
1. It will not interact with N server, highscores cannot be loaded / uploaded.
2. It will not affect your original N userdata, it stores your progress in another position
3. It does not contain the engine of N. So I suppose it does not violate the user license.
4. It cannot run as a standalone program. It needs your N file to run, which I won't provide.
5. It affects the drone data externally after your N file is being loaded by it.
6. It is _your_ decision to put it in to the same folder of N-Flash-Player-8 (in that case you already violated the license? ) and is _your_decision to run it

*About making a first highscore post*

Long time ago, L3X has posted his first 20th and asked for comments. Many top players have kindly given him comments and feedback so that he can improve. And now L3X has became an expert of N.

If, it will be good to have more skilled players in N, then, why don't we encourage more newbie players to post their first highscore, and then tell them the way to improve? More and more skilled players can be trainned by us.

As long as we keep helping the new highscorers to improve themselves, they will all become skilled players one day.

*About L3X posts*

Look at how competition starts:
http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=12690

I simply enjoy L3X throws a challenge out, and then other highscorers come and challenge each others.

This can facilitate the innovation of new routes, this can facilitate the competitions. By seeing how each highscorer clean up different parts of the route, other newbies & new highscorers can then learn a lot.

For me, I'm just a newbie, I want to learn, I want to know the reason of how every single new route got improved.

*Conclusion*

If you want to eliminate all these posts, you are building a wall to stop a lot of potential players to become great.

New Feature: Recover Level Highscore from online server to local sol

If you have lost your sol, and want to recover a level highscore from online server, you can hold the "Ctrl" button while you download your own online highscore replay. Hold the Ctrl button until you found your local score being updated.

note:
1. you can only restore your own highscore.
2. It won't work if you don't have an online highscore for that level
3. It only restore the highscore if your online level highscore is higher than your local one. So you don't need to worry about it replacing your local better data.

Metanet Forums writes:

>
> Raigan,
>
> Unreality has sent you this email from http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php.
>
>
> Dear Raigan,
>
> Since highscorers are very concern about the problem of speedhacking (Players like kryx-orange has runied the highscores), and I know some flash codings, so I have built a N version that can detect speedhack. (Note: I haven't changed a single line of code inside N, and I haven't converted the N.swf into fla file.)
>
> You can find the speedhack detection enabled N in this topic:
> http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=7561&st=280
>
> To detect speedhack with flash actionscript, I compare the different between "getTimer()" and "new Date()".
>
> getTimer() return the flash time, while new Date() returned the OS time.
> If a flash is being speedhacked, the time-passed calculated from getTimer() will be different from new Date().
>
> Here's the actionscript for speedhack detection:
>
> -----------------------------------------
> now = new Date();
> vStartTime = now.getTime();
>
> onEnterFrame = function() {
>      now = new Date();
>      vDiff = now.getTime()-vStartTime-getTimer();
>      trace(vDiff);
>      // 1000 millisecond seems a acceptable number to be legit
>      if (vDiff>=1000) {
>              vError =  "Your Flash Player might have been running with an abnormally slow speed";
>      }
> }
> -----------------------------------------
>
> It is of highscorers wish to have speedhack detection built in N. Please kindly read this topic if you have the time. Many thanks.
> http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=7561&st=280
>
>
> Regards,
> Unreality
>
> ---------------------------------------------------
> Please note that Metanet Forums has no control over the
> contents of this message.
> ---------------------------------------------------
>
>
> Regards,
>
> The Metanet Forums team.
> http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php
>

#17344 1 2007-09-27T18:24:21Z Unreality
Are you hungered for more levels to highscore? Do you want to compete Legacy packs with your friends and all N players in the world online?

With the love of N, I have created this add-on to the current N.

NReality6c is ready for testing now. Please report any bugs that you have found. And Please backup your sol file first.
http://nrealitypages.googlepages.com/NReality6c2.zip

It has an additional 100 episodes to the game.
It has added two new modes for the game:
Speedrun mode & slowdown mode

column 10th - 11th:
Blue_tetris super hard column 1 & 2

column 12th - 19th
Legacy Original Pack
{
Column 0 - tktktk
Column 1 - LittleViking001
Column 2 - nevermore
Column 3 - maximo
Column 4 - Kablizzy
Column 5 - Kablizzy
Column 6 - maximo
Column 7 - tktktk
}

It has epi 100 to 199 online highscores support at my highscores server.
Please report to me for any bad names/cheated scores!

All scores which are 0th or past 0th will have a "*" in front of the player name of that score

Latest top 20 episode/level scores
http://n.infunity.com/latest_ep_score.php
http://n.infunity.com/latest_lv_score.php

You can view a player highscores here:
http://n.infunity.com/user_score.php?name=[user name]
http://n.infunity.com/ep_score.php?name=[user name]
e.g.
http://n.infunity.com/user_score.php?name=mr_lim
http://n.infunity.com/ep_score.php?name=mr_lim

You can use NHighX from OptionalBowser to check the online highscores:
http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=19667

background:
Quote
<LittleViking> Hey, Unreality, I forwarded a PM to you from Raigan.
<LittleViking> It looks like they're okay with your mod if you use a different highscore server, and they'd prefer if you package it in an exe to help protect against hacking.

It blocks the direct usage of speedhack

It supports online level highscore recovery (by ctrl click your online score).

v6c2 features:
1. Shift click your local personal level run will pop up a browser containing the demo text
2. Supports foreground image with different blend modes (look for more here: http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=21904 look for image maps here: http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=21871 )
3. Supports drone attributes modification

v6b Bug fixed:
1. Fixed a score submission bugs. Now all version 5a,5b,6a,6b scores should be able to submit without any problems. You can re-submit your version 4 runs, but version 4 runs will always be ranked lower than version 5+ runs.

v6a Bugs fixed & features added:
1. Fixed the bug of non-login & different username score submission
2. Added foreground image for custom map. To add it, put "|background_pic_url|foreground_pic_url" in front of the last # of the map data, like:
#mapdata.......................................|background_pic_url|foreground_pic_url#
Image dimension is 792x600, please note that the top left of the foreground image will cover the first row of the map, while the background image will be covered by the first row of the map.
3. Support png/gif/jpg format for custom fore/background images.
4. You can try speedmode even if you haven't cleared 6 columns. To try underclock mode you still need to clear 6 columns.
5. All local version 4 or older runs will be replaced with new runs.
6. NReality server now host its own legit episode 0-99 highscores.
7. NReality server has removed all episode 100-199 version 4 runs, to ensure that all runs in NReality server is legit.
8. It doesn't load your userlevels.txt anymore. In userlevel screen, you can type the numa map ID, and then that numa map will be loaded for you.
9. ctrl-click your custom map highscores will submit your custom map highscores to NReality server, and then the ranking table of that map will be prompted.

v5b Bug fixed:
1. Fixed episode score update bug.
2. Fixed flavours unlock bug.

v5a Feature added:
1. Custom web background picture for user level: add "|http://www.yourdomain.com/yourpic.jpg" just before the last # of your map data. You can only use jpg as the picture format.
2. Added two game modes: Speed-run and underclock mode. For speed-run mode, the less frames your run, the better your scores. For underclock mode, you can play levels with FBF fashion.
3. Online highscores & speedrun & underclock ranking tables.
4. Goddy enhanced security.
5. Episode highscores recovery, level highscores recovery, and episode/level beaten recovery. All done by Ctrl-clicking your online top 20 highscores in the game.
6. Frame data display

v4 bugs fixed:
1. fixed the problem that forced you to clear the entire episode to have the levels unlocked.
2. fixed the problem that caused the levels of last episodes impossible to be unlocked.
3. The grid button is not tab-able now.

Disclaimer:
1. There is no N source code recompilation. I haven't modified the programming code of N. My add on made use of the N functions externally and attach additional episodes onto it.

2. I WILL NEVER STORE YOUR N PASSWORD at my highscores server.

** For those who loves this add-on, please shows your support thanks

p.s. You can check your unsubmitted score by using NReality Score Comparer http://n.infunity.com/downloads/NRealityScoreComparer.zip

#17399 1 2007-10-02T06:00:40Z Unreality
I'd like to add more episodes to my N add-on
http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=19543

Currently I have ideas for 4 columns:

1. A whole column of pure N tricks. (e.g. tktktk simple challenge, St Atilla's College for Ninja: http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=9751 , or other ppl's simple challenge )

2. A whole column of pure racing map, where the ninja keep the momentum and never needs to stop/slow down (is this kind of map called racing map?)

3. A whole column of pure puzzles that allow different routes (maps like blue_tetris "time is on my side")

4. A whole column of extreme metanet levels (pick those very famous metanet levels from epi 0-epi99, and make them extremely hard or extreme weird.... e.g. add 10 laser drones in 00-0, or "pick 5 most difficult levels and remove all the golds and put them in an episode")

so the themes for these 4 columns are:

1. the N learner

2. the N racer

3. the N thinker

4. the metanet fanatics

I have these four categories in mind because I want the new episodes to satisfy different taste of highscores.

I have a lot more ideas tho, but I don't have that much time I still need to code more scripts on my highscores database, so I will need help in picking / building the levels. And I want help from serious highscorers, since if you don't highscores then you can just play the maps in numa with "userlevel mode"

I also want the columns to be made one by one carefully. So let's focus on the column of pure N tricks first.
Atilla has an awesome collection of N tricks here (~45 levels): http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=9751
And tktktk also has his simple challenges (18 levels):
http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=19330

I don't have a fast comp (nor time) to test all of these levels yet. It will be great if someone can arrange them into 10 episodes.

Since this column is for learning N tricks, so I think it is okay to keep the level be short and simple.
Also I'd like to know if the Atilla maps have covered all of tktktk simple challenge tricks.

If all the maps of Atilla & tktktk has their own uniqueness, then maybe it will be good to combine 2 levels into 1 level, like the left hand side of the room is for trick 1 and then right hand side of the room is for trick 2.

The idea is to pack ALL tricks into the column, so maybe we should keep a blank episode for tricks that found in the future, and keep the right to edit and change the levels.
i.e. All levels in this column are subjected to change! Like the textbooks in school are updated to catch up the new theories.

If you are interested, please kindly make a reply and tell us your thought, thanks.

#372933 48 2007-10-19T01:41:21Z Unreality
I have a different opinion on the "max-out" definition

to me, highscorers are of two types. One type will go for 0th, one type will go for top 20th

my definition of "max-out" of a level = those "0th types highscorers" have already tried their best to get the highest score, and it is already very hard to innovate new routes for that level.

there's some tricks of the "0th type highscorers" that "20th type highscorers" can never copy. e.g. There are only very very few ppl who can do the 58-1 high jump (wedgie, mr_lim, toti, johnny_fancea), not even pokemaniac can do the jump.

so to me, even if there are only 10 highscorers on a level, as long as those "0th type highscorers" have tried their best on it, then the level is considered max-out for me.

If the new 100 episodes are maxed-out in this way, then I think that I can add new columns.

The reason I said this, is because those "0th type highscorers" have real passion (and patient) to play the game. If all of them have already tried their best on the levels, but still required to wait those "20th type highscorers" to fill in the blank (especially my mod is not that well-known), then it is just a waste of time. Better provide them new episodes earlier.

Of course, I know xaelar & mr_lim still far from trying their best on many of the 100-199 epi levels

#387118 611 2007-12-07T10:26:16Z Unreality
Quote: Fenneth, December 07, 2007 06:20 pm
Unreality, will it be possible to turn that off if we wish?
It is a part of the map design. It is the map creator to decide whether to put a background image to a level or not. It won't affect other levels that you play.
If you don't like that user level, then don't play it, or you can remove the bg image string in that map data string. What's the point of playing an userlevel if you dislike the settings from the map creator?

To add a bg image to a map, just add a 
|[image url]
before the last "#" of a map data

#391447 23 2007-12-20T06:57:53Z Unreality

We are not taking away certian aspects. In fact the normal ranking mode keep all the good aspects of the original game

We have a speedrun mode, and it is very similiar to the normal highscores mode, just players compete for least frames instead of highest score

NReality has also got a underclock mode, which you can say that it has taken away certain aspects of the highscoring challenge. So, actually we are "adding" new aspects plus keeping the old aspects in good health

#391458 29 2007-12-20T08:17:44Z Unreality
Quote: Infernus, December 20, 2007 03:55 pm
I have a question. You stated:

"1. There is no N source code recompilation. I haven't modified the programming code of N. My add on made use of the N functions externally and attach additional episodes onto it."

If so, why is there an n_v14.swf in the package of your mod? I'm no expert, but doesn't this violate the license?

I am dissapointed you included the "underclock" mode. While it may not directly harm other highscore tables, it may encourage players to use similar slowdown methods in the original n, indirectly harming the tables.

Other than that, the improvements you have made aren't really going to improve the original tables, but is likely to be good competition for the legit players.

for matter 1:
Firstly n_v14.swf comes from n_v14.exe without recompilation. Secondly I am told by littleviking that M&R permit the development of this project. Thirdly I have updated M&R about the whole program and they know about this. I have asked whether I need to add/remove/edit anything for NReality, and they don't tell me to add/remove/edit anything yet.

for matter 2:
Most highscorers here know about cheat engine. It is a known problem.
Many of the highscorers used cheat engine to test out possible routes/runs first, and then retry the runs in normal speed. When they found that they are unable to execute the same run in normal speed, they were tempted to submit the cheated scores.

For this reason I provided the underclock mode, which they can freely test out the runs in slow motion and submit to the underclock ranking table. They no longer need to use cheat engine, they can also let other ppl know about the possible runs that they invented.

The map author can set the map so that only the author can see all the top 20 scores, while other players can only view their own scores.

If the contest host is not the author, then the contest host needs to submit a duplicated map as his map to numa. Of course he should write in the map description about the original map author

If the contest host wants to end the round, he can ask the players to stop submitting new runs (otherwise disqualify), or he can simply remove the exit from the map

Private contest runs can't be visible to other ppl, even if the contest has ended.
By the way, it is not hard at all for the contest host to copy the top 20 runs demo data out.


Here is a screenshot of the ranking table implemented inside the game
http://n.infunity.com/images/NReality6cPreview2.jpg

#393778 1031 2007-12-27T12:58:14Z Unreality
Version 6c is ready for testing, you can download it at here:

http://n.infunity.com/downloads/NReality6c2.zip

Remember to backup your sol file first. This is a testing version only. Once the bugs are gone (if any), then I will call it NReality6d and release it officially.

v6c Bugs fixed & features added:
*0. Most important update: The Highscores is splitted into two versions. The default one is the "Original Mode", which highscores episodes 0-99 will load the metanet server scores tables. Another mode is "Legit Mode", if you want to change to "Legit Mode", please edit "LegitMode.txt" and set the content from "0" to "1". Once "Legit Mode" is on, it will load the NReality Server scores table for highscores episodes 0-99, and the version 4 scores will be ranked lower. Also, when "Legit Mode" is on, your old version 4 runs will be replaced with any new runs that you make. In both modes, when you submit episode 0-99 highscores , the scores will be submitted to both Metanet server & NReality server. Please note that Metanet server scores only go up, so nothing to be worried about. NReality server, on the other hand, rank legit runs first, those version 4 runs will be always put under legit runs.
1. Now the game uses your own runs as the demo runs.
2. Now demo runs will display episode number and level number. The first demo run has a problem displaying its episode & level tho.
3. Userlevels screen support both user level and numa map. It first loads in userlevels.txt. The runs of userlevels map will not be able to submit to NReality server. You can load numa map by typing numa map ID and click the load numa map button.
4. Now it also shows numa map thumbnail and ranking table inside the userlevels screen.
5. You can set a numa map as "privatecontest" map, which the players can only see his own scores, only the map author can see all players scores.

v6c Bugs fixed & features added:
*0. Most important update: The Highscores is splitted into two versions. The default one is the "Original Mode", which highscores episodes 0-99 will load the metanet server scores tables. Another mode is "Legit Mode", if you want to change to "Legit Mode", please edit "LegitMode.txt" and set the content from "0" to "1". Once "Legit Mode" is on, it will load the NReality Server scores table for highscores episodes 0-99, and the version 4 scores will be ranked lower. Also, when "Legit Mode" is on, your old version 4 runs will be replaced with any new runs that you make. In both modes, when you submit episode 0-99 highscores , the scores will be submitted to both Metanet server & NReality server. Please note that Metanet server scores only go up, so nothing to be worried about. NReality server, on the other hand, rank legit runs first, those version 4 runs will be always put under legit runs.

if you don't want to overwrite your higher version 4 runs, please don't use legit mode.... legit mode is for people to prove how high they can get in absolute legit environment.
When you have the confidence to pass or tie or get close to your old score, you can switch legit mode on, so that you can make a legit run and submit it to the server.

added the "locked" map type for numa map

add |||locked to the end of the map data, then the server will not accept new scores.
But since the map data string is provided from the flash client, so if the user loaded the map before you added the "locked" type, then the user will still able to submit the score unless he closed NReality or reloaded the map

#402321 24 2008-01-20T14:32:12Z Unreality

= means the run ties the current 0th
* means the person had the 0th at one point in time where there are at least 10 runs in the table
. means it may not be a legit run

A lot of defenses against speedhack and decompilation has been introduced to NReality starting from version 5, only runs that are made from NReality version 5+ are considered 100% legit. All other runs will have a "." before it

#410181 420 2008-02-17T16:49:06Z Unreality
I have an interesting idea for a new highscores contest that makes use of the 'private contest' feature.

It's called 'Destiny'. This contest can be as long as four weeks, but also can be as short as one week.

The map of the 1st week will have 4 areas, and each area will contain its exit and switch. Each area has its own theme which should fit different types of highscorers.

Players can pick any of the exit to make the demo. At the end of the week, only the players of the highest score of each exit survive.
(i.e. if you think that xaelar will probably pick exit A, then you better pick exit B to avoid direct contest with him, it is a mind game hahaha)

After the 1st week, there can be at most 4 players left. For the 2nd week, the map will only have (players count minus one) areas, therefore at the end of 2nd week, there can be at most 3 players left. Same with 3rd and 4th week.

For the map creator, he should have the sense and skill to create map for different kind of highscorers

I post my idea here because I think players of Blur suit this kind of contest

#414815 11 2008-03-08T01:46:20Z Unreality
to get the gold on 87-1 with one free drone,

1. touch the trap button to block the bottom drone first.

2. then when you enter the middle area, the top drone will shoot at the top gap, where you can freely get the lower golds first

3. Then you can jump jump jump jump to the top golds, if you are fast enough and get close to the top drone enough and jumping at the right time, the top drone will then shoot just at the top of your head and you can get all the gold near it.

I believe if I can do it, then most highscorers here can easily do it. And since I have successfully done it before, so it should be easy

#422294 1427 2008-03-27T12:25:16Z Unreality
The awesomeness of NReality knows no bound.

Now the foreground of NReality supports different blendmodes!

This is a transparent update where you don't need to download anything to have this happened.

The default NReality map has the inverted blendmode applied.
Please take a look at the bottom left corner of the ninja, half of the body is inverted to white color, and half of the body is remained to be black



There are 1 to 14 blend modes, description copied from flash help

1
"normal"

The movie clip appears in front of the background. Pixel values of the movie clip override those of the background. Where the movie clip is transparent, the background is visible.

2
"layer"

Forces the creation of a temporary buffer for precomposition for the movie clip. This is done automatically if there is more than one child object in a movie clip and a blendMode setting other than "normal" is selected for the child.

3
"multiply"

Multiplies the values of the movie clip constituent colors by those of the background color, and then normalizes by dividing by 0xFF, resulting in darker colors. This setting is commonly used for shadows and depth effects.

For example, if a constituent color (such as red) of one pixel in the movie clip and the corresponding color of the pixel in the background both have the value 0x88, the multiplied result is 0x4840. Dividing by 0xFF yields a value of 0x48 for that constituent color, which is a darker shade than that of the movie clip or that of the background.

4
"screen"

Multiplies the complement (inverse) of the movie clip color by the complement of the background color, resulting in a bleaching effect. This setting is commonly used for highlights or to remove black areas of the movie clip.

5
"lighten"

Selects the lighter of the constituent colors of the movie clip and those of the background (the ones with the larger values). This setting is commonly used for superimposing type.

For example, if the movie clip has a pixel with an RGB value of 0xFFCC33, and the background pixel has an RGB value of 0xDDF800, then the resulting RGB value for the displayed pixel is 0xFFF833 (because 0xFF > 0xDD, 0xCC < 0xF8, and 0x33 > 0x00 = 33).

6
"darken"

Selects the darker of the constituent colors of the movie clip and those of the background (the ones with the smaller values). This setting is commonly used for superimposing type.

For example, if the movie clip has a pixel with an RGB value of 0xFFCC33, and the background pixel has an RGB value of 0xDDF800, the resulting RGB value for the displayed pixel is 0xDDCC00 (because 0xFF > 0xDD, 0xCC < 0xF8, and 0x33 > 0x00 = 33).

7
"difference"

Compares the constituent colors of the movie clip with those of its background, and subtracts the darker of the values of the two constituent colors from the lighter one. This setting is commonly used for more vibrant colors.

For example, if the movie clip has a pixel with a RGB value of 0xFFCC33, and the background pixel has an RGB value of 0xDDF800, the resulting RGB value for the displayed pixel is 0x222C33 (because 0xFF - 0xDD = 0x22, 0xF8 - 0xCC = 0x2C, and 0x33 - 0x00 = 0x33).

8
"add"

Adds the values of the constituent colors of the movie clip to those of its background, applying a ceiling of 0xFF. This setting is commonly used for animating a lightening dissolve between two objects.

For example, if the movie clip has a pixel with an RGB value of 0xAAA633, and the background pixel has an RGB value of 0xDD2200, the resulting RGB value for the displayed pixel is 0xFFC833 (because 0xAA + 0xDD > 0xFF, 0xA6 + 0x22 = 0xC8, and 0x33 + 0x00 = 0x33).

9
"subtract"

Subtracts the values of the constituent colors in the movie clip from those of the background, applying a floor of 0. This setting is commonly used for animating a darkening dissolve between two objects.

For example, if the movie clip has a pixel with an RGB value of 0xAA2233, and the background pixel has an RGB value of 0xDDA600, the resulting RGB value for the displayed pixel is 0x338400 (because 0xDD - 0xAA = 0x33, 0xA6 - 0x22 = 0x84, and 0x00 - 0x33 < 0x00).

10
"invert"

Inverts the background.

11
"alpha"

Applies the alpha value of each pixel of the movie clip to the background. This requires the "layer" blendMode setting to be applied to a parent movie clip. For example, in the illustration, the parent movie clip, which is a white background, has blendMode = "layer".

12
"erase"

Erases the background based on the alpha value of the movie clip. This requires the "layer" blendMode to be applied to a parent movie clip. For example, in the illustration, the parent movie clip, which is a white background, has blendMode = "layer".

13
"overlay"

Adjusts the color of each bitmap based on the darkness of the background. If the background is lighter than 50% gray, the movie clip and background colors are screened, which results in a lighter color. If the background is darker than 50% gray, the colors are multiplied, which results in a darker color. This setting is commonly used for shading effects.

14
"hardlight"

Adjusts the color of each bitmap based on the darkness of the movie clip. If the movie clip is lighter than 50% gray, the movie clip and background colors are screened, which results in a lighter color. If the movie clip is darker than 50% gray, the colors are multiplied, which results in a darker color. This setting is commonly used for shading effects.

To apply blendmode to the foreground image, just add a ?[blendmode] to the end of image url
e.g.
http://img480.imageshack.us/img480/4760/ichigo28bh.png?10

You can load the default NReality map 111791 and look at the map data for reference
#422295 14 2008-03-27T12:28:25Z Unreality
This is a post copied from NReality main thread:

The awesomeness of NReality knows no bound.

Now the foreground of NReality supports different blendmodes!

This is a transparent update where you don't need to download anything to have this happened.

The default NReality map has the inverted blendmode applied.
Please take a look at the bottom left corner of the ninja, half of the body is inverted to white color, and half of the body is remained to be black



The blend mode can affect the ninja, as well as drones tiles gold mines and everything underneath the foreground image.

There are 1 to 14 blend modes, description copied from flash help

1
"normal"

The movie clip appears in front of the background. Pixel values of the movie clip override those of the background. Where the movie clip is transparent, the background is visible.

2
"layer"

Forces the creation of a temporary buffer for precomposition for the movie clip. This is done automatically if there is more than one child object in a movie clip and a blendMode setting other than "normal" is selected for the child.

Dividing by 0xFF yields a value of 0x48 for that constituent color, which is a darker shade than that of the movie clip or that of the background.

4
"screen"

"darken"

10
"invert"

Inverts the background.

11
"alpha"

Applies the alpha value of each pixel of the movie clip to the background. This requires the "layer" blendMode setting to be applied to a parent movie clip. For example, in the illustration, the parent movie clip, which is a white background, has blendMode = "layer".

12
"erase"

Erases the background based on the alpha value of the movie clip. This requires the "layer" blendMode to be applied to a parent movie clip. For example, in the illustration, the parent movie clip, which is a white background, has blendMode = "layer".

13
"overlay"

14

If the movie clip is lighter than 50% gray, the movie clip and background colors are screened, which results in a lighter color. If the movie clip is darker than 50% gray, the colors are multiplied, which results in a darker color. This setting is commonly used for shading effects.

#422986 19 2008-03-29T04:44:29Z Unreality
Since ppl are bombing my pm box, let me release the info:

[0] means zap drones, [1] means lasers and [2] means chaingun

r for radius

to have several effects put together (I haven't tested it tho):

0,r,20;1,r,5;2,r,100

Other drone attributes, which I haven't tested (I have only tested r radius):

speed for speed
fireDelayTimer (default 0)
prefireDelay (default 0)
postfireDelay (default 0)
laserPrefireDelay (default 30)
laserPostfireDelay (default 40)
laserRate (default 80)
laserLen (default 7)
chaingunPrefireDelay (default 35)
chaingunPostfireDelay (default 60)
chaingunMaxNum (default 8)
chaingunRate (default 6)
chaingunSpread (default 3.000000E-001)


so the code can be like

0,speed,100;1,laserPrefireDelay,1;1,laserLen,30


I haven't really tested them tho.... gotta go out now

Currently there's no way to edit other objects yet, as I haven't added the code yet.
and I do not know whether it is possible to edit other objects yet, as I haven't spent the time to 'investigate'

#19751 1 2008-03-30T14:34:28Z Unreality
Motherlode on Moon, experience low gravity here
http://numa.notdot.net/map/120522

All credits go to M&R for making such a great game.

This player mod is built for those map makers who love the original N

For those who used NReality map, please add the tag "NReality"

For those who used image, please add the tag "image-map"
For those who used drone mod, please add the tag "droneMod"
For those who used player mod, please add the tag "playerMod"

WARNING WARNING WARNING WARNING
Most N players adapt to the origina Ninja Control. Any modification to the relation between ninja and the environment could create hateful playing experience.
My concern is, especially to the highscorers, if there are too many nreality playermod map which alter the way they control the ninja, they may feel more reluctant to try new playermod map.
A successful game usually is a game where those skillful player can precisely and accurately control how the avatar (ninja here) response. If you change the ninja attribute here, it may lower the overall playing experience of N.
WARNING WARNING WARNING WARNING

NReality Map data structure:

$MAPNAME#AUTHOR#TYPE#leveldata|objectdata|bgImage|fgImage|NRealityMapType|droneMod|playerMod#


Here are the attribute list, which I haven't tested all of them, please help me to test them one by one and report the result :

r
maxspeedAir , default = r * 5.000000E-001;
maxspeedGround, default = r * 5.000000E-001;
groundAccel, default = 1.500000E-001;
airAccel, default = 1.000000E-001;
normGrav, default = 1.500000E-001; I suggest you to set g equal to the same value too. e.g. normGrav,1;g,1
jumpGrav, default = 2.500000E-002;
normDrag, default = 9.900000E-001; I suggest you to set d equal to the same value too. e.g. normDrag,2;d,2
winDrag, default = 8.000000E-001;
wallFriction, default = 1.300000E-001;
skidFriction, default = 9.200000E-001;
standFriction, default = 8.000000E-001;
g, default = normGrav;
d, default = normDrag;
jumpAmt, default = 1;
jump_y_bias, default = 2;
max_jump_time, default = 30;
terminal_vel, default = r * 9.000000E-001;

#444181 7 2008-06-13T10:29:51Z Unreality
The only possible way for Flash AS2.0 to download a file (or virus) is using getURL, not LoadVars as used in download map data as well as N Highscores.

There is a getURL in NReality coding that link to http://n.infunity.com/

There is also a getURL in original N coding, that link to metanet site

9=DRONE, oh yea use this one if you want to messed up 0,2,3
10=OneWayPlatform
11=Thwump
12=TestDoor (TestDoor? I don't know it's called TestDoor)
13=HomingLauncher
14=Exit
15=Mine

here's come with the parameters
3=gold
r only, default = tiles.xw * 5.000000E-001;

4=BounceBlock
stiff = 5.000000E-002;
mass = 2.000000E-001;
sleepThreshold = 40;
sleepTimer = 0;

5=LAUNCHPAD
r = tiles.xw * 5.000000E-001;
strength = tiles.xw * 4.285714E-001; <== I believe this will be handy

6=TURRET
closeAimSpeed = 5.000000E-002;
midAimSpeed = 3.500000E-002;
farAimSpeed = 3.000000E-002;
aimSpeed = this.farAimSpeed;
outerThreshold = tiles.xw * 8;
innerThreshold = tiles.xw * 2;
midThreshold = 2.500000E-001 * this.outerThreshold + 7.500000E-001 * this.innerThreshold;
outerThreshold = this.outerThreshold * this.outerThreshold;
midThreshold = this.midThreshold * this.midThreshold;
innerThreshold = this.innerThreshold * this.innerThreshold;
shotRate = 60;
shotTimer = 0;
fireDelayTimer = 0;
prefireDelay = 10;
postfireDelay = 10;

7=FLOORGUARD
r = tiles.xw * 5.000000E-001;
speed = tiles.xw * 4.285714E-001;

10=OneWayPlatform
um... seems nothing can edit... not even r

11=Thwump (in my mind this object = Blue_Tetris)
Um a lot of settings, I think the only viable settings are:
xw = tiles.xw * 7.500000E-001;
yw = tiles.xw * 7.500000E-001;
fallspeed = tiles.xw * 3.571429E-001;
raisespeed = tiles.xw * 1.428571E-001;
speed = this.fallspeed;

12=TestDoor
oh i'm amazed this one is the most confusing, lots of parameters inappropriate to edit
doorsize = 10; <== i guess this one won't work
r = tiles.xw * 8.333333E-001; <== i guess this one won't work too
maxtimer = 5;

13=HomingLauncher
speed = 0;
maxspeed = tiles.xw * 2.857143E-001;
startaccel = 1.000000E-001;
curaccel = this.startaccel;
accelrate = 1.100000E+000;
turnrate = 1.000000E-001;
isHoming = false;
prefireDelay = 10;
fireDelayTimer = 0;

14=Exit
r = tiles.xw; <== it doesn't affect the trigger radius tho

15=Mine
r = tiles.xw * 3.333333E-001;

Yay happy testing

#449887 63 2008-07-01T03:24:34Z Unreality
Sorry for double post (I honestly don't think double post used up that much server resources tho), but this is a critical info to the mod:

you can only input Numbers as the value. You can input "3", "0.5" etc, but if you put "tiles.xw * 5.285714E-001" etc the value will become zero.

i.e. "6,closeAimSpeed,1" is correct,
but not "6,closeAimSpeed,5.000000E-002",
not "6,outerThreshold,tiles.xw * 8"

Update

http://nmaps.net/129343 <== redrum, redrum, redrum, red drone!!!!!!!

Now NReality supports the following modification to the graphics(movieclip in terms of flash) of the drones/objects:

_xscale ( in %, e.g. 0,_xscale,50 will return a 50% width drone, graphical only, not the 'actual size')
_yscale ( in %)
_alpha (transparent from 0 to 100%)

and

_color
this is not a native flash attribute, I introduce it to the clip, you can set the color like this :
0,_color,100.100.0.0.0.0.50.0
which made the zap drone become red and semi transparent

as you can see, the values are connected with dot "."
red%.redMod.green%.greenMod.blue%.blueMod.transparent%.transparentMod

% are from -100 to 100
Mod can range from -255 to 255 I think... seems can be more than that

so
0,_color,100.100.0.0.0.0.50.0
remain its original 100% red, and add 100 to its red, and removed all its original green and blue, plus making it 50% transparent

Keep in mind this update only affect the drone/object appearance, and probably won't affect its ACTUAL SIZE.

As you know, when you scale the drone/object very big with a big "r", the graphics seems to be out of scale, at this moment you can use _xscale and _yscale to fix that.

#20699 1 2008-07-03T17:59:42Z Unreality
NReality Rotational Laser Drone

http://nmaps.net/129802

laser done code:
6^x,y,?,0,1,?
to turn it to rotational laser drone, make it:
6^x,y,?,1,1,?
i.e. replacing the 4th attribute from 0 to 1

moddable attributes: (like laser drone it used 1 as object #)

startingAngle (default = 0, it is the very beginning shooting angle)
rotateSpeed (default = 0.5, if you put it -0.5 it will shoot anti-clockwise)
fixedLaserLength (default not exist, you can set it 100 to have a look, the laser will have fixed length and can strike through wall, it will have a very weird feeling)

the collision detection between the rotational laser and the player isn't perfect.. if you are fast enough you may pass thr the laser, but it's too hard for me to make it perfect
#450529 5 2008-07-03T18:38:17Z Unreality
I just made it much harder to pass through the laser, as long as the rotateSpeed is not set to a large number

N+ Force Field Drone! Now available in NReality

http://nmaps.net/130843

Restart NReality (a mod of N, d/l @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Force Field Drone

Force Field Drone : 6^x,y,Path,0,16,direction

optional parameters: ^detectRadius,forcePower,forceDelayTimer,forceMaxCount,forceMaxCountDelay

Default:
detectRadius = 100
forcePower = 4
forceDelayTimer = 10 (The time gap between the pushes, if you are still in range)
forceMaxCount = 3 (The number of times the drone feel tired and need to rest for a bit)
forceMaxCountDelay = 200 (rest for 200 frames)

Some N+ players please kindly check if this force field drone behaves the same like the N+ one, many thanks.

Alert!!!!Please notice that this Force Field Drone is subjected to change. It is not final. If you make maps with it, the maps & demos may break.

Haunted House 2 (Ghost Drone Type 2)

http://nmaps.net/130949

Must play the original Haunted House first:
http://nmaps.net/130910

Restart NReality (a mod of N, d/l @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Roboboo Ghost Drone Type 2

Ghost Drone 2 : 6^x,y,Path,0,102,direction

optional parameters: ^acceleration,maxvelocity,minAlpha

Default:
acceleration = 0.02
maxvelocity = 2
minAlpha = 0(default zero, set it higher to make it not totally invisible when you face them)

The original Ghost Drone will follow you when you are not looking at them. They are always harmful.

The Ghost Drone Type 2 will become invisible and harmless when you are looking at them, that means you can run through them. They are only harmful when you are not looking at them.

#452584 14 2008-07-13T04:40:49Z Unreality
Quote: The Kitchen Sink, July 13, 2008 12:34 pm
You can make what is essentially one of those pinball knockers by adding

^20,7,2,999,1

After the end of each, and making sure they don't move. 20 is about the size of the drone itself, 7 you can change since it's just it's power (default 6), 2 is the interval between repulsions, and by then you should be out of there anyways, 999 is how many times it must be hit before it temporarily powers down, and 1 is the interval between power downs. Basically whenever you touch it, it repels you at the force specified. It's a lot like a jump pad, but it depends on which angle you touch it at.

ALSO! Nreality, now not only does the code vanish when you press L to load, but when going into the editor the drones are *DELETED*. Entirely. It's not good. I would be perfectly fine with them just looking like zap drones or even just being invisible on the editor, but downright deleting them is awful. That needs to be fixed.

The Ned is a nasty thing to modify I know its inconvenient, but I suggest that you finish the tiles and zap drones first, and at the end replace the zap drones with new drones

4^x,y,1^ 0,20

#20796 1 2008-07-14T16:32:03Z Unreality
I believe this is the most revolutionary update I have ever made.

I mod N for real usage, I don't mod N just for personal favourite features. Try this map and you will understand

Play And Stop (Triggered Objects)

http://nmaps.net/131345

I implemented the Trigger Functions on the objects, so that you can play and pause individual object by touching the corresponding door trigger.

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Triggered Objects

To add "Trigger" to a door switch, append
^startTriggers(separated by "."),stopTriggers(separated by ".") to the door switch object (which starts with 9^ )

To make the object triggerable, append
^^waitForTrigger,startTriggers(separated by "."),stopTriggers(separated by ".")

For an example, the first line of drones can be started by the triggerID 1, and can be stopped by trigger ID 2
so their codes are
!6^180,180,3,0,0,2^^,1,2!

The door switch which starts it has the code of
!9^228,276,0,0,27,11,1,-1,0!
and the door switch which stops it has the code of
!9^228,240,0,0,2,11,1,0,0^,2!

The boo is initially stopped, and can be started with trigger ID 5, so it has a code of
!6^756,276,3,0,101,2^****,5!

BooBoo Cave (Custom Path)
http://nmaps.net/131461

Yeah Yeah Yeah Everyday we have a new Update!

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Custom Path

CustomPath for Drone:
6^x,y,6,0,droneType,direction^^^customPath

where customPath is formed with U (up) D (down) L (left) R (right)

e.g. in my code:
!6^252,492,6,0,121,3^^^RRRLLLLLRR!
This drone will move right 3 tiles, then left 5 tiles, then right 2 tiles, and repeat and repeat and repeat

#453032 2 2008-07-15T16:46:31Z Unreality
To ensure NReality works:

1. Clear all your temporary internet files or cache of all of your browsers (firefox + ie + opera etc)

2. Restart NReality

3. Wait for the top left "Transparent Update finished loading" text disappear

4. then the newest transparent update is loaded, you can feel free to play the games

#453195 12 2008-07-16T06:21:59Z Unreality
Quote: numa_ninja, July 16, 2008 12:21 pm
Can someone please tell me what's wrong with this code? The triggers are working in reverse. The 1 trigger stops the Boo drone, and the 2 trigger starts it.

!6^636,252,3,0,103,1^75,100,2!6^252,444,3,0,121,1!9^552,144,1,0,27,23,1,0,-1!9^95,580,1,0,27,23,1,0,-1

Hi please kindly check the trigger drone coding:
^^waitForTrigger,startTriggers(separated by "."),stopTriggers(separated by ".")

So the first parameter is waitForTrigger

if you want the it to be start with trigger 1 and stop with trigger 2

you can put ^,1,2 where you ignored the first parameter

if you want the drone to be initially stopped, you can put

****,1,2



like

!6^636,252,3,0,103,1^75,100^,1,2!

My PlayGround (AI Pathing Objects)

http://nmaps.net/131853

Here is the new update Sorry for the delay, but I found it quite hard to implement this function

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of AI Pathing Objects

All objects except rocket launcher and floor guard can be applied for Drone's AI Pathing system on it.

AI Pathing Objects : append
^^^customPath,PathID,Direction,speed

Path ID:
SURFACE FOLLOW Clock Wise = 0;
SURFACE FOLLOW Counter Clock Wise = 1;
WANDER Clock Wise= 2;
WANDER Counter Clock Wise = 3;
WANDER ALTERNATING = 4;
WANDER RANDOM = 5;
Nreality CustomPath = 6;
Circular Movement = 7;

Such as the gold used in this map:
!0^732,60^^^,2,2,2!

I have also added circular movement for objects. It will use objects' original position as center x,y
PathID is 7
^^^,7,0,speed,startAngle,Radius

Such as the mine in this map:
!12^396,276^****,1^,7,0,-2,270,100!

I was not awared that undefined variables cause great lag in Flash

like

onEnterFrame = function () {
if (drone.customPath != xxxxx) {
execute drone.customPath
}
}

if drone.customPath is undefined, it is MUCH slower.

So I go and defined all undefined variables, now the flash is much smoother

MineZigZagMine
http://nmaps.net/132735

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Coordinates Pathing

Coordinates Pathing (Path ID 8) : append the following to the drones/objects

#20908 1 2008-07-28T15:02:04Z Unreality
Cloud Chamber (SpecialTrigger+ChaseAI)
http://nmaps.net/132939

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Special Trigger and chasing Pathing AI

To add "Special Trigger" to a door switch, append
^startTriggers(separated by "."),stopTriggers(separated by "."),startSpecialTriggers(separated by "."),stopSpecialTriggers(separated by ".") to the door object

To make the object special triggerable, append
^^waitForTrigger,startTriggers(separated by "."),stopTriggers(separated by "."),startSpecialTriggers(separated by "."),stopSpecialTriggers(separated by ".")

zap drone -> specialOn -> seeker drone
seeker drone -> specialOff -> zap drone

rotating laser drone:
specialOn-> keep rotating
specialOff->stop rotating

Chasing Pathing (Path ID 9) : append the following to the drones/objects
^^^,9,direction,speed
The drone/object will chase the ninja at all time

Such as the rocky drone in this game:
!6^84,84,2,0,121,0^****,6^,9,0,1!

#20910 1 2008-07-28T16:28:32Z Unreality
Bahahaha (TextDrone)
http://nmaps.net/132946

Bahahahaha I opened a new genre for mapping. A genre of comic

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of TextDrone

Text Drone : 6^x,y,Path,1,201,direction^textArray,x_offset,y_offset,align,border,textColor,backgroundColor,fontSize,font,bold,italic,underline

Apart from textArray, all other paramters are optional

textArray: escaped text characters joined by ":"
align: 0=left, 1=center, 2=right
border: 0= no border, 1=has border
textColor & backgroundColor: from 000000 to FFFFFF
font: _sans, _serif, _typewriter , if you put other fonts, other players may not able to see it
bold,italic,underline : 0 or 1

specialOn Trigger: Display next text in array
SpecialOff Trigger: Hiding the text

escape your text:
http://n.infunity.com/swf/escape.swf
Note: Non english characters will not be able to be shown on all users computers

#20945 1 2008-07-30T14:11:33Z Unreality
Forum [?] (Custom Icon)
http://nmaps.net/133174

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Custom Icon

To set custom Icon to objects, used
||||,_icon,x_offset^y_offset^url

Please kindly beware that in N, all the object icon size are scaled down by default, so the image you loaded will probably be scaled down too. You can set _xscale,100 and _yscale,100 to restore your image default size.
The eye movieclip of the drone will disappear if you change its icon.

Since the image loaded into the object will not auto center itself, so you probably need to set x_offset and y_offset to adjust the correct position of the image to the object

e.g. the zap drone here
||||0,_icon,-10^-10^http://metanet.2.forumer.com/style_images/Invision_Power_Board/icon14.gif;0,_xscale,100;0,_yscale,100;

#20976 1 2008-08-01T05:36:56Z Unreality
Left & Right (OneWay On/Off + Rotate)
http://nmaps.net/133401

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of one way platform on/off + rotation

To work with one way platform on/off & rotation, you need to use special triggers (ref: http://nmaps.net/132939 )

oneway special off : make the one way disappear

oneway special on:
case 1: if the one way has disappeared, it will reappear
case 2: if the one way is not disappeared, then it will rotate 90 degree

In this map you can see how it works, and when you touched the 2nd door trigger, the first oneway disappear, and when you touched the 3rd door trigger, the first oneway reappear again.

#456998 3 2008-08-01T06:31:34Z Unreality
example:
oneway:
!7^228,564,3^^,,,1
trigger:
9^228,564,0,0,9,23,1,0,0^,,1

to rotate it two times
oneway:
!7^228,564,3^^,,,1.2
trigger:
9^228,564,0,0,9,23,1,0,0^,,1.2

to rotate it three times

oneway:
!7^228,564,3^^,,,1.2.3
trigger:
9^228,564,0,0,9,23,1,0,0^,,1.2.3

#457534 1860 2008-08-02T10:35:37Z Unreality
Quote: squibbles, August 02, 2008 05:21 pm
I'm afraid I don't understand the special trigger...
what is seperated by a "."?
What do the numbers represent when doing this?

---------------------------------
On a seperate topic, it would be nice if a switch could be intergrated to change the value of a mod...for example, normal sized ninjas running along, when he hits a switch... suddently r = 5 and he can fit inbetween all the 5 tiles.

suppose a drone is initially stopped, its code will be

6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****

if you want it a it to be able to be triggered by a door switch, then you have to assign a trigger ID to it, say ID 3

6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,3

you will then also need a door to activate it using ID 3

9^.........****

Then when you touch the door switch, the drone will be activated

Suppose you also want another door switch able to trigger the drone, and the other door switch trigger ID is 4

then the codes will be
6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,3.4
9^.........****
9^.........^4

Suppose the first door switch will also trigger another drone with ID 5, then the codes will be

6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,3.4
6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,5
9^.........****.5
9^.........^4

=========================================================

Currently it is impossible to change the ninja attribute in the middle of the run. I don't know if this is possible or not yet.
#457543 1862 2008-08-02T11:14:50Z Unreality
Oh my previous post only explained the trigger, not special trigger

let me continue the content with the previous example of

6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,3.4
6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,5
9^.........****.5
9^.........^4

suppose you want the first drone be stoppable by the 2nd door switch with ID 6, then it will be

6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,3.4,6
6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,5
9^.........****.5
9^.........^4,6

Suppose you want the first zap drone to be able to change into a seeker drone by the first door switch, with special Trigger ID 7, then it will be

6^x,y,path,0,0,direction^****,3.4,6,7
6^x,y,path,chaser,droneType,direction^****,5
9^.........****.5,,7
9^.........^4,6

Suppose the 2nd drone is a seeker drone, and you want it to turn into a normal zap drone by touching the 2nd door switch using special trigger ID 8, then it will be

6^x,y,path,0,0,direction^****,3.4,6,7
6^x,y,path,1,0,direction^****,5,,,8
9^.........****.5,,7
9^.........^4,6,,8

Please notice the differences between . and ,

greedy drone will work for the function of "touching too long" = dead

there maybe two types of countdown drone.
Type A: once you touched it, there will be a timer on top of your head counting down, once it reached 0 you will die, but if you touched Type B drone again, your timer will be reset (it will be customizable for the timer & reset-able / non reset-able)
Type B: once you touched it, there will be a timer on top of the drone counting down, once it reached 0 it will blown up the whole screen, but if you hit it again the drone timer will be reset. So if you touched multiple Type B Countdown drones at different areas, you will be very busy keeping all of them from blowing up the screen.

Time delay for trigger... I haven't thought of any easy way to implement this yet.

Song and sounds.... not at the moment.

#21027 1 2008-08-05T06:01:20Z Unreality
Drain me up (Greedy Drone)
http://nmaps.net/133855

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Greedy Drone

Greedy Drone:
6^x,y,Path,0,141,direction^speed,chase_speed,collect_coin
Greedy Seeker Drone:
6^x,y,Path,1,141,direction^speed,chase_speed,collect_coin

For collect_coin:
value for stealing 1 second = -40
value for stealing 1 frame = -1
value for giving player 1 second = 40
This value must be an integer

Greedy drone -> specialOn -> seeker Greedy drone
seeker Greedy drone -> specialOff -> Greedy drone

Updates to Zap Drone & Rocky Drone
Zap Drone:
6^x,y,Path,1,0,direction^speed,chase_speed
Rocky Drone:
6^x,y,Path,1,121,direction^speed,chase_speed

So you can also set chase speed for zap and rocky drone now

Also for rocky drone:
rocky drone -> specialOn -> seeker rocky drone
seeker rocky drone -> specialOff -> rocky drone

NReality Road Map For Unreality
If you have any suggestion please post at http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=23327

Possible to implement on next update:
* Door special on & off to open & close doors again

Likely be implement in a few weeks
* Stop & Pause for Custom Pathing
* Stop for coordinates pathing
* blinking drone
* teleportor drone
* drone with weakness

Likely to be implement in a few months
* Highscoring survival maps, NReality will use the frame count as score.
* Setting frame count on exit switch, which will have a timer countdown when the ninja touches it, once the countdown reached 0 you won the level.
* Zap doors
* Countdown Drone Type A: once you touched it, there will be a timer on top of your head counting down, once it reached 0 you will die, but if you touched Type B drone again, your timer will be reset (it will be customizable for the timer & reset-able / non reset-able)
* CountDown Drone Type B: once you touched it, there will be a timer on top of the drone counting down, once it reached 0 it will blown up the whole screen, but if you hit it again the drone timer will be reset. So if you touched multiple Type B Countdown drones at different areas, you will be very busy keeping all of them from blowing up the screen.
* total 0th & '=' & '*' count at n.infunity.com
* Slightly better Ned
* Oneway platform that rotate every ? frames.
* New columns (Legacy & NReality columns)
* better userlevel interface
* Ranking for total score of 1st 100 episode & 2nd 100 episodes
* New custom paths
* individual object mod
* runs with AGD will have an "a" marked in front of the score
* Next major NReality version that has the mods packed, so that you don't need to d/l transparent update to make the mod ready


Already implemented in next major version
* Practise mode score bug fixed for numa & userlevels

Likely to be implement in far future
* Chat

Things that I wish to implement, but hard for me
* One life challenge
* Multiple ninjas on same local computer
* Multiplayer
* Combining highscore & speedrun mode

Currently Impossible
* New Sound / custom bg mp3
* Perfect Ned
* new tiles
* diagonal oneway
* changing player/ninja/object attribute in the middle of the game

#459479 122 2008-08-09T09:53:16Z Unreality
>it's velocity is altered accordingly, but will not stop as it does when it hits a launchpad or a FFD

So it adds the velocity to ninja's velocity? I can consider this

>* Force Beam Drone

Wait till you see the alarm drone

>I know you've said it would be hard, but Ned could do with an overhaul.

Although I am good at flash programming, but I extremely hate to code user interface (hahahaha). And editing Ned is even harder for me, it has two reasons why its hard

1. the Ned interface graphics are set inside the N original source. The interface is not generated by codings. NReality can mod the codings easily, but it is hard to mod the already finished graphical interface in original N

2. Ned interface is not designed to have extra parameters for objects. It doesn't assume the mapper got the power to edit its attribute. If you want a Ned that supports editing attribute, then it maybe easier to ask some other programmers to create a whole new editor
#21065 1 2008-08-09T15:26:58Z Unreality
MotherLode Secured (Alarm Drone)
http://nmaps.net/134406

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Alarm Drone

Alarm Drone:
6^x,y,Path,0,132,direction
Alarm Seeker Drone:
6^x,y,Path,1,132,direction
Optional Parameter
^speed,chase_speed,laserMinRadius,laserMaxRadius,radiusUp,radiusDown,neverDown,alwaysMove

laserMinRadius, the min radius of the laser, default 30
laserMaxRadius, the max radius of the laser, default 400
radiusUp, the increase rate of the radius, default 1
radiusDown, the decrease rate of the radius, default 2
neverDown, if it is set to 1 then the laser will never decrease in size
alwaysMove, if it is set to 1 then the drone will move even when they see you, like the seeker alarm drone in this map

If both of the radiusUp & radiusDown are set to negative, then the laser radius will decrease when it see you and increase when it doesn't see you

specialOff : Lock the radius, so no more size change in laser radius
specialOn : allowed size change for laser radius

To create your first mod map:

Make an empty map, place one ninja, and place one zap drone, then copy the map data out to a text file

#459825 129 2008-08-11T07:01:51Z Unreality
Quote: mattk210, August 11, 2008 01:55 pm
Has a post been deleted? I could have sworn I posted. Anyway the gist was that one way diagonals could be implemented using the slope code from 45deg tiles because the reportCollisionVsWorld function only needs to take projection and tile normal vectors which can be sent from any non grid-aligned position. The collision would be checked by comparing last frame's collision with this frame, and if the ninja moves from in front of the tile to behind the tile in 2 consecutive frames, this is where you apply the projection code.

I also mentioned that this would cause slight bugs almost identically to axis-aligned one ways, because it doesn't cater for vertices.

If you want I could make a quick mockup, it's really not very hard.

I also asked what are the issues you had with the things you'd "like to implement but are too hard for you".

Which post? you have a post which I have quoted and answered.

Seems you are much more familiar with the N physics than me, it will be great if you can help me in this part while I can focus on other areas like building more drones

The collision with oneway is a bit different because oneway doesn't care the ninja consecutive frames. As you know original oneway got some 'bug' as 'feature' (like you can jump thr one way in chimney), I think we should not bring in the tile collision detection to the oneway.

Anyway what's your suggestion on updating the one way code so that it can supports diagonal collision?

OneWayPlatformObject.prototype.TestVsPlayer = function (guy)
{
    var _loc3 = guy.pos;
    var _loc7 = _loc3.y - this.pos.y;
    var _loc9 = this.yw + guy.yw - Math.abs(_loc7);
    if (_loc9 > 0)
    {
        var _loc8 = _loc3.x - this.pos.x;
        var _loc10 = this.xw + guy.xw - Math.abs(_loc8);
        if (_loc10 > 0)
        {
            if (this.dir.x == 0)
            {
                var _loc4 = guy.pos.y - guy.oldpos.y;
                if (_loc4 * this.dir.y <= 0)
                {
                    var _loc11 = guy.oldpos.y - this.dir.y * guy.yw - (this.pos.y + this.dir.y * this.yw);
                    if (_loc11 * this.dir.y >= 0)
                    {
                        var _loc5 = this.pos.y + this.dir.y * this.yw - (guy.pos.y - this.dir.y * guy.yw);
                        guy.ReportCollisionVsObject(0, _loc5, 0, this.dir.y, this);
                    } // end if
                } // end if
            }
            else
            {
                _loc4 = guy.pos.x - guy.oldpos.x;
                if (_loc4 * this.dir.x <= 0)
                {
                    _loc11 = guy.oldpos.x - this.dir.x * guy.xw - (this.pos.x + this.dir.x * this.xw);
                    if (_loc11 * this.dir.x >= 0)
                    {
                        var _loc6 = this.pos.x + this.dir.x * this.xw - (guy.pos.x - this.dir.x * guy.xw);
                        guy.ReportCollisionVsObject(_loc6, 0, this.dir.x, 0, this);
                    }
                }
            }
        } 
    } 
};

Time is a factor of hardness. Some features take a lot of time to implement so it will be hard. for an example, multiplayer
#459891 133 2008-08-11T14:39:39Z Unreality
Um sorry maybe you haven't actually modded n so you haven't faced the limitation that N has.

I have to reuse the oneway and cannot create new object ID as numa will block unidentified objects

The TestVsPlayer function is called from the player when the player interacts with nearby objects. So it means that the testvsplayer function can be called in frame 1 and frame 3 but not frame 2. Calling the TestVsPlayer at every frame for all oneway will lag the game.
But I believe your function will be usable by replacing this.cache with the game tick timer to identify the collision frame. So yes the diagonal oneway is implentable

By the way I believe you have no experience in making flash multiplayer games... there are so many problems about it that you seem you haven't considered If you have ever made any multiplayer flash games you will know.

One of the many issues will be that Flash can only connect and cannot listen to socket connections.
The other one is about lagging issue, how you synchronize things between two players
There is no perfect solution, if there is, then it just come down to logically implement them. But since there is no perfect solution so it comes to how you handle the non-ideal world In N+ you see the networked ninja hit a mine and die and after a second the ninja is not dying from hitting the mine. Even N+ has this synchronization issue, I would very much like to hear your solution to this problem (Although I have my own approach to these two and many other multiplayers related problems)

#21134 1 2008-08-17T14:28:17Z Unreality
MotherLode Guarded (Chase AI 2)
http://nmaps.net/135355

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Chasing Pathing AI 2

Chasing Pathing 10 (Path ID 10) : append the following to the drones/objects
^^^,10,direction,speed,chaseWhenSighted

The drone/object will first set its goal position as ninja current position, then once the object reached that goal position, it will reset its goal position again.

Such as the zap drone in this map:
6^588,492,10,0,0,1^^^,10,0,1

chaseWhenSighted: default undefined. Set it to 1 when you want it to chase the ninja only when the it 'sees' the ninja, such as the alarm drone in this map
6^300,84,0,0,132,0^,,,,,,,1^^,10,0,4,1

I personally think that this chase AI combined with alarm drone, makes the ninja act like more an assassin

#21240 1 2008-08-25T15:49:48Z Unreality
I wanna be the guy (Jump Settings)
http://nmaps.net/136251

This is the third room of I wanna be the guy
Watch the video http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uvL3Vxn1g-8 here you can find out the third room

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Jump Settings

noWallJump, default 0, set to 1 to disable wall jump
noWallSlide, default 0, set to 1 to disable wall slide
maxJumpCount, default 1, set to 2 to enable double jump (i.e. you can jump in air), set to 3 to enable triple jump and so on, set to 0 to disable floor jump (you can still wall jump, to disable wall jump too set noWallJump to 1)
jumpResetYSpeed, default 0, set to 1 to reset coordinate Y speed when jumping, please test this newly added attribute, cause I don't know how to explain it. (Test it with double jump and you will get the idea)

The mod in this map:
|||||noWallJump,1;noWallSlide,1;maxJumpCount,2;maxspeedAir,2

#465440 1898 2008-08-29T17:34:27Z Unreality
L4byrinth (Area Trigger)
http://nmaps.net/136604

Restart NReality (a mod of N, download @ n.infunity.com) to retrieve this new update of Area Trigger

Area Trigger:
Placed after the playerMod "|", i.e. the sixth "|"
||||||^x0.y0:x1.y1^startTriggersID(separated by "."),stopTriggersID(separated by "."),specialOnTriggersID(separated by "."),specialOffTriggersID(separated by ".")

(x0,y0) and (x1,y1) formed the rectangle area that trigger things

you can put multiple area triggers, separated by ";"

e.g. to make the ninja trigger the greedy drone when enter the top left area in this map and stop it when the ninja back to center cross:
!6^372,276,2,0,141,0^,,-10,1,5.6^,9,0,4||||||!5^250.150:384.286;!5^250.286:540.314^,5;!5^384.150:410.430^,6

#465881 41 2008-08-31T07:37:13Z Unreality
When I released the playermod, I do aware that some people will probably make bad maps

I have put serious warnings in my player mod post http://metanet.2.forumer.com/index.php?showtopic=22072

But to me I believe I have no right to hold back the player mod.

I should not be the one who decide what the map makers should able to do and what the map makers should not able to do

Instead, it should be the numa ratings and user comments to teach the map makers about what a good map and what a bad map is.

There are not many player modded map so far. Many of them are bad, which already got bad ratings and comments. Some of them are good, which already got good ratings and comments.

Since there are only a few and limited player modded maps existed, I think if you dislike them you can easily avoid them as there are so many many other maps out there.

Same to other mods, if you dislike those mods, you can avoid them as there are so many other choices out there.

For double jump mod, some players like it, some players don't.
What if I take away this mod? I will take away the fun from those people who like this mod. I don't want to do that.

Say, if there is a new innovative idea, that I know quite some players will like it, I will feel bad if I don't let those who like it to enjoy the new mod.

Please understand how NReality works with original n_v14.swf

NReality loads in n_v14.swf and changes the functions inside.

Ad while I appreciate your interface design to my max extent, I afraid some of the interface you designed is impossible to implement.
